Bhanunamikaze/Agentic-SEO-Skill: test run, 2026-11-12

The same task for every tool: audit a public copy of the test shop at https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/ (2026-11-12), the same pages as the sandbox one, with 16 planted SEO problems. Claude Code (Claude Opus 5.5) installed the tool from its README and ran its audit; a judge (Claude Opus 5.5, three passes, given the whole site) marked the report, and every false claim was checked by hand. Repo commit 445c46c268d9. All results · The test site · Task

Found 16 of 16 planted problems; 2 false claims about the site; 44 problems raised in all; 18 min.

It refuses private addresses, so it ran on 2026-11-12 on a public copy of the same site; Claude Code with no tool found all 16 there too. It took the /shop/ redirect for a duplicate of /teas/ and counted no product links on /teas/, which has two.

The planted problems

Planted problemResult
D1 /robots.txt
robots.txt has `Disallow: /blog/`, so crawlers may not fetch the whole blog, although the blog pages are in the sitemap and linked from every page.
“robots.txt blocks the whole Journal (/blog/) although both blog URLs are listed in the sitemap”		found
D2 /teas/jasmine-silver-needle/
The best-selling product page has <meta name="robots" content="noindex, follow">, so it is kept out of search results although it is in the sitemap and linked from the home page.
“Product page has meta robots noindex and is listed in the sitemap — /teas/jasmine-silver-needle/”		found
D3 /teas/
The tea category page's canonical points to the home page (http://lumentea.test/) instead of itself.
“Category page /teas/ canonicalises to the homepage, so the main product listing is dropped from the index”		found
D4 /blog/how-to-brew-green-tea/
The blog article has no <title> element at all.
“Blog post has no <title> element”		found
D5 /teas/ and /blog/
/teas/ and /blog/ have the same meta description (duplicate descriptions).
“Meta description duplicated from /teas/ (describes the tea catalogue, not the Journal)”		found
D6 /blog/how-to-brew-green-tea/
The article links to /teas/dragon-well/, which answers 404 (broken internal link).
“Broken internal link: 'see Dragon Well' points to a 404 page”		found
D7 /sitemap.xml
The sitemap lists /teas/white-peony/, which answers 404.
“Sitemap lists a URL that returns HTTP 404 (also noindex) — /teas/white-peony/”		found
D8 /teas/jasmine-silver-needle/
The product page's Product JSON-LD is not valid JSON (a trailing comma), so the structured data cannot be parsed.
“Product JSON-LD is invalid JSON (trailing commas), so no Product/Offer structured data is parsed”		found
D9 /teas/jasmine-silver-needle/
The product page's main product image has no alt attribute.
“Product image missing alt text — /teas/jasmine-silver-needle/”		found
D10 /contact/
The contact page has no H1 heading.
“Missing H1 — /contact/”		found
D11 site navigation
The 'Shop' link in the navigation on every page goes to /shop/, which redirects 301 to /store/, which redirects 301 to /teas/ (a redirect chain on an internal link).
“Redirect chain: /shop/ → /store/ → /teas/ (2 × 301), linked from the sitewide nav”		found
D12 /blog/
The blog index page has no <meta name="viewport"> tag (not mobile friendly).
“Journal index has no viewport meta tag”		found
D13 /teas/bai-mudan/
The Bai Mudan product page is sent with an HTTP header X-Robots-Tag: noindex, so it is kept out of search results; its HTML has no robots meta, so only the response header shows it.
“Product page is noindexed by an X-Robots-Tag HTTP header (invisible in HTML) and is listed in the sitemap — /teas/bai-mudan/”		found
D14 /guides/storing-tea/
/guides/storing-tea/ is an orphan page: it is in the sitemap and answers 200, but no page on the site links to it.
“Orphan pages: in the sitemap but zero inbound internal links — /guides/storing-tea/”		found
D15 / (home)
The home page declares <link rel="alternate" hreflang="zh-CN" href="http://lumentea.test/zh/">, and /zh/ answers 404 (a broken hreflang alternate; there is also no x-default or self reference).
“hreflang alternate points to /zh/, which returns 404 and has no return tag”		found
D16 / and /guides/storing-tea/
/guides/storing-tea/ has the same <title> as the home page (duplicate titles).
“Duplicate title: uses the homepage title — /guides/storing-tea/”		found

False claims (checked by hand)

  • /shop/ and /teas/ serve identical content (duplicate content); duplicate title, meta description and H1 between /shop/ and /teas/
    /shop/ serves no content of its own. It redirects (301 → /store/ → 301 → /teas/), so there is no duplicate page. The redirect chain is the real problem.
  • Few crawlable product links detected (0) on /teas/
    /teas/ has two plain crawlable <a> links, to /teas/jasmine-silver-needle/ and /teas/bai-mudan/.

The report

report.md
# SEO Audit Report (seo skill)

**Site:** https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/ · **Date:** 2026-11-11 · **Tool:** `seo` skill (`~/.claude/skills/seo`), full-audit workflow (`seo-audit.md` + `llm-audit-rubric.md`)

## A) Audit Summary

- **Scope:** full-site. 13 URLs examined: the 10 sitemap URLs, plus `/shop/`, `/teas/dragon-well/` and `/zh/`, which were found through links or hreflang. Evidence came from `audit_runner.py`, `crawl_audit.py`, `sitemap_checker.py`, `indexability_matrix.py`, `canonical_checker.py`, `x_robots_header_checker.py`, `hreflang_checker.py --verify-returns`, `broken_links.py` (every live page), the per-page schema/E-E-A-T/citation/freshness/a11y/mobile/social/image scripts, `validate_schema.py` and `finding_verifier.py`.
- **Business type detected:** E-commerce (a small loose-leaf tea shop in Bristol, UK, with product pages, a category page and a journal).
- **Scores:**
  - Automated dashboard (`audit_runner.py` / `SEO-REPORT.html`, script weights, mostly homepage checks): **71/100 (Good)**, score confidence Medium.
  - Rubric chain-of-thought score across the whole site (SKILL.md weights; Performance is left out because there was no data): Technical 0 · Content 9 · On-Page 19 · Schema 5 · Images 45 · AI Search 23 · Performance: insufficient data. The weighted result is **about 12/100 (Critical)**, score confidence **Low**. The low number comes from several indexing blockers on money pages. The rubric's −15 per Critical penalty drives the score to the floor.
- **Top issues:**
  1. Both product pages are noindexed. Jasmine Silver Needle uses a meta robots tag; Bai Mudan uses an `X-Robots-Tag` HTTP header.
  2. `/teas/` (and `/shop/`, which redirects to it) canonicalises to the homepage.
  3. robots.txt `Disallow: /blog/` blocks the whole Journal from crawling.
  4. The Jasmine Silver Needle Product JSON-LD is invalid JSON.
  5. The sitemap lists a 404 (`/teas/white-peony/`), and the hreflang points to a 404 (`/zh/`).
- **Top quick wins:**
  1. Remove the `/blog/` disallow.
  2. Remove the noindex directives.
  3. Make `/teas/` self-canonical.
  4. Fix the JSON-LD trailing commas.
  5. Add a `<title>` to the blog post.
  6. Fix the broken Dragon Well link.
  7. Point the nav "Shop" link at `/teas/`.

## Verifier notes: contradicted script output (listed, not counted)

The skill's verifier step (`seo-verifier` role + `finding_verifier.py`) suppresses findings that the evidence contradicts. These are listed here so that nothing the tool raised is hidden:

| Page URL | Tool / raw severity | Raw finding | Why suppressed |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/`, `/about/`, `/teas/`, `/shop/`, `/teas/jasmine-silver-needle/`, `/teas/bai-mudan/`, `/blog/how-to-brew-green-tea/`, `/contact/`, `/guides/storing-tea/` | `mobile_render_checker.py` / critical | "Missing or incomplete viewport meta tag." | The raw HTML of each of these pages contains `<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">`, and `a11y_seo_checker` passes them. The checker misreads the parsed meta. The finding is genuine **only** on `/blog/` and on the 404 template, and both of those are kept in the table above. |
| `/contact/` | `broken_links.py` / 🔴 | Link to `/` reported as broken (`connection_failed`) | It was a transient network error. A single retry showed 0 broken links. |

## D) Unknowns, follow-ups and environment limitations

- **Core Web Vitals / PageSpeed:** Google's PageSpeed API returned *rate limited* on the first run and again on one retry, so no LCP, INP or CLS data was measured. Performance is reported as "Insufficient data", not as a site issue. Rerun `pagespeed.py` with `PAGESPEED_API_KEY`.
- **Visual analysis:** `capture_screenshot.py` and `analyze_visual.py` could not run because the Playwright Chromium browser is not installed. There are no screenshots and no above-the-fold analysis.
- **`article_seo.py`:** crashed with a script bug (`AttributeError: 'list' object has no attribute 'get'` when the JSON-LD is a list). Article/keyword analysis is incomplete. This is not a site issue.
- **Search Console / analytics / keyword data:** none exists for this new site, so no ranking, traffic or keyword claims are made.
- **Score caveat:** the dashboard score (71) mostly reflects homepage checks. It does not weight the sitewide indexability blockers found by the crawl/indexability scripts.

- `FULL-AUDIT-REPORT.md`, `ACTION-PLAN.md` — the skill's mandatory deliverables (LLM-first synthesis)
- `SCRIPT-FULL-AUDIT-REPORT.md`, `SCRIPT-ACTION-PLAN.md`, `SEO-REPORT.html` — auto-generated by `audit_runner.py` (homepage-centred script run)
- `raw/` — every script's JSON output, fetched HTML, raw and verified findings
ACTION-PLAN.md
# Action Plan — lumentea-test.vercel.app

Generated by the `seo` skill (full audit). Ordered Critical → High → Medium → Low, per `seo-audit.md` priority definitions.

## Critical (6)

- **robots.txt blocks the whole Journal (/blog/) although both blog URLs are listed in the sitemap** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/blog/ ; https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/blog/how-to-brew-green-tea/`  
  Severity: Critical · Confidence: Confirmed · Fix: Remove `Disallow: /blog/` from robots.txt
- **Product page has meta robots noindex and is listed in the sitemap** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/teas/jasmine-silver-needle/`  
  Severity: Critical · Confidence: Confirmed · Fix: Remove noindex from the product page
- **Product page is noindexed by an X-Robots-Tag HTTP header (invisible in HTML) and is listed in the sitemap** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/teas/bai-mudan/`  
  Severity: Critical · Confidence: Confirmed · Fix: Remove the X-Robots-Tag noindex header for this path (Vercel headers/config)
- **Category page /teas/ canonicalises to the homepage, so the main product listing is dropped from the index** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/teas/ (and https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/shop/ which redirects to it)`  
  Severity: Critical · Confidence: Confirmed · Fix: Make /teas/ self-canonical
- **Product JSON-LD is invalid JSON (trailing commas), so no Product/Offer structured data is parsed** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/teas/jasmine-silver-needle/`  
  Severity: Critical · Confidence: Confirmed · Fix: Remove the trailing commas after `availability` and after `offers` and re-validate
- **hreflang alternate points to /zh/, which returns 404 and has no return tag** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/ → https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/zh/`  
  Severity: Critical · Confidence: Confirmed · Fix: Remove the zh-CN alternate until a Chinese version exists

## High (10)

- **Sitemap lists a URL that returns HTTP 404 (also noindex)** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/teas/white-peony/`  
  Severity: Critical · Confidence: Confirmed · Fix: Remove from sitemap, or 301 it to /teas/bai-mudan/ (same tea)
- **Broken internal link: 'see Dragon Well' points to a 404 page** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/blog/how-to-brew-green-tea/ → https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/teas/dragon-well/`  
  Severity: Critical · Confidence: Confirmed · Fix: Fix or remove the link (no Dragon Well product exists)
- **No self-referencing hreflang tag** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/`  
  Severity: Critical · Confidence: Confirmed · Fix: Add self-referencing hreflang (or remove hreflang altogether)
- **Orphan pages: in the sitemap but zero inbound internal links** — `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/guides/storing-tea/ ; https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/teas/white-peony/`

FULL-AUDIT-REPORT.md
# Full SEO Audit Report

**Site:** https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/ · **Date:** 2026-11-11 · **Tool:** `seo` skill (`~/.claude/skills/seo`), full-audit workflow (`seo-audit.md` + `llm-audit-rubric.md`)

## A) Audit Summary

- **Scope:** full-site. 13 URLs examined: the 10 sitemap URLs, plus `/shop/`, `/teas/dragon-well/` and `/zh/`, which were found through links or hreflang. Evidence came from `audit_runner.py`, `crawl_audit.py`, `sitemap_checker.py`, `indexability_matrix.py`, `canonical_checker.py`, `x_robots_header_checker.py`, `hreflang_checker.py --verify-returns`, `broken_links.py` (every live page), the per-page schema/E-E-A-T/citation/freshness/a11y/mobile/social/image scripts, `validate_schema.py` and `finding_verifier.py`.
- **Business type detected:** E-commerce (a small loose-leaf tea shop in Bristol, UK, with product pages, a category page and a journal).
- **Scores:**
  - Automated dashboard (`audit_runner.py` / `SEO-REPORT.html`, script weights, mostly homepage checks): **71/100 (Good)**, score confidence Medium.
  - Rubric chain-of-thought score across the whole site (SKILL.md weights; Performance is left out because there was no data): Technical 0 · Content 9 · On-Page 19 · Schema 5 · Images 45 · AI Search 23 · Performance: insufficient data. The weighted result is **about 12/100 (Critical)**, score confidence **Low**. The low number comes from several indexing blockers on money pages. The rubric's −15 per Critical penalty drives the score to the floor.
- **Top issues:**
  1. Both product pages are noindexed. Jasmine Silver Needle uses a meta robots tag; Bai Mudan uses an `X-Robots-Tag` HTTP header.
  2. `/teas/` (and `/shop/`, which redirects to it) canonicalises to the homepage.
  3. robots.txt `Disallow: /blog/` blocks the whole Journal from crawling.
  4. The Jasmine Silver Needle Product JSON-LD is invalid JSON.
  5. The sitemap lists a 404 (`/teas/white-peony/`), and the hreflang points to a 404 (`/zh/`).
- **Top quick wins:**
  1. Remove the `/blog/` disallow.
  2. Remove the noindex directives.
  3. Make `/teas/` self-canonical.
  4. Fix the JSON-LD trailing commas.
  5. Add a `<title>` to the blog post.
  6. Fix the broken Dragon Well link.
  7. Point the nav "Shop" link at `/teas/`.

SCRIPT-ACTION-PLAN.md
# Action Plan

- URL: `https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/`
- Overall score: `71/100`

## Priority Fixes

1. **5 security headers missing**
   - Priority: `Critical`
   - Area: `environment`
   - Evidence: Missing headers reduce trust and can expose the site to browser/security risks.
   - Fix: Set missing security headers at web server or CDN layer.
2. **2 orphan page(s) with zero inbound internal links.**
   - Priority: `Critical`
   - Area: `link_profile`
   - Evidence: See audit output.
   - Fix: Add internal links from relevant content pages to these orphan pages.
3. **Entity schema exists but has no sameAs properties.**
   - Priority: `Critical`
   - Area: `sameAs`
   - Evidence: See audit output.
   - Fix: Add sameAs URLs pointing to Wikipedia, LinkedIn, Twitter/X, etc.
4. **No llms.txt found**
   - Priority: `Warning`
   - Area: `environment`
   - Evidence: AI crawlers and assistants have no curated machine-readable guidance for key pages.
   - Fix: Add `/llms.txt` at site root with concise site description and key URLs.
5. **No Wikidata entry found for 'Lumen Tea Co.'.**
   - Priority: `Info`
   - Area: `Wikidata`
   - Evidence: See audit output.
   - Fix: If the entity meets Wikidata notability guidelines, create or improve an item with accurate third-party references. Do not create one solely for SEO.
6. **No Wikipedia article found for 'Lumen Tea Co.'.**
   - Priority: `Info`
   - Area: `Wikipedia`
   - Evidence: See audit output.
   - Fix: Only pursue Wikipedia if the entity meets independent notability standards. Otherwise, strengthen official schema, sameAs profiles, citations, and About/Contact signals.
7. **Performance measurement incomplete**
   - Priority: `Info`
   - Area: `environment`
   - Evidence: PageSpeed API returned an error, so CWV recommendations are less reliable.
   - Fix: Set `PAGESPEED_API_KEY` in your environment or `.env` file (see `.env.example`), then rerun. The CLI also accepts `--api-key`. Prioritize LCP/INP/CLS fixes from that output.
8. **Missing sameAs link to Wikipedia (Primary KG signal).**
   - Priority: `Info`
   - Area: `sameAs`
   - Evidence: See audit output.
   - Fix: Add the existing official 'wikipedia.org' URL to sameAs; do not create this profile solely for SEO.
9. **Missing sameAs link to Wikidata (Primary KG signal).**
   - Priority: `Info`
   - Area: `sameAs`
   - Evidence: See audit output.
   - Fix: Add the existing official 'wikidata.org' URL to sameAs; do not create this profile solely for SEO.
10. **Missing sameAs link to LinkedIn (Strong KG signal).**
   - Priority: `Info`
   - Area: `sameAs`
   - Evidence: See audit output.
   - Fix: Add 'linkedin.com' profile URL to sameAs array in your entity schema.
11. **Missing sameAs link to Twitter/X (Strong KG signal).**
   - Priority: `Info`
   - Area: `sameAs`
   - Evidence: See audit output.
   - Fix: Add 'x.com' profile URL to sameAs array in your entity schema.

We started Lumen Tea in 2021 because the white tea we could buy in the UK was usually two or three years old by the time it reached a cup.</p>\n<h2>Where our tea comes from</h2>\n<p>We buy from three farms: the Lin family in Diantou, the Chen family near Fu'an and a cooperative of eleven growers in Panxi. None of them sells through a wholesaler. We pay before harvest, which lets them pick by hand and skip the machine-cut autumn crop. Each lot is tasted in Fujian and again in Bristol, and we turn down about one in three.</p>\n<h2>What we do not do</h2>\n<p>We do not add flavourings, sell blends or use tea bags. The only scented tea we sell is jasmine, and it is scented with real flowers over several nights, the traditional way. If a tea does not sell within six months of harvest, we give it to staff and friends instead of discounting it.</p>\n<p>Questions about a tea or an order? <a href=\"/contact/\">Write to us</a>; one of us answers every message within a working day.</p>\n</main>\n<footer><p>Lumen Tea Co., 18 Harbour Lane, Bristol BS1 4RW, United Kingdom \u00b7 <a href=\"/about/\">About us</a> \u00b7 <a href=\"/contact/\">Contact</a> \u00b7 <a href=\"/teas/\">All teas</a></p><p>\u00a9 2026 Lumen Tea Co.</p></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n",

tea leaves unfurling in a glass teapot\" width=\"1200\" height=\"800\">\n<p>Most bitter green tea is brewed with water that is too hot or left on the leaf too long. Green tea is not roasted or oxidised the way black tea is, so boiling water pulls out tannins and caffeine fast and cooks the delicate flavours. Get the temperature and the time right and nearly any decent green tea tastes sweet.</p>\n<h2>Water temperature</h2>\n<p>Brew most green teas at 75 to 80 \u00b0C. Without a thermometer kettle, boil the water and let it stand with the lid open for about four minutes, or add one part cold water to four parts boiling. Delicate Chinese greens such as Dragon Well, which we hope to stock next spring (<a href=\"/teas/dragon-well/\">see Dragon Well</a>), like the lower end of that range.</p>\n<h2>How much leaf and how long</h2>\n<p>For a 250 ml mug, use 2 to 3 grams of leaf, about a heaped teaspoon, and steep for 2 minutes. For a teapot, scale up: 5 grams for 500 ml. In a gaiwan, use 4 grams for 100 ml and pour off after 30 seconds, adding 10 seconds for each infusion after that.</p>\n<h2>When the cup is wrong</h2>\n<ul><li>Bitter: the water was too hot or the tea steeped too long. Cool the water or shorten the time, not both at once.</li>\n<li>Thin or flat: too little leaf. Add more leaf before you lengthen the time.</li>\n<li>Grassy and sharp: common with Japanese greens; try 70 \u00b0C.</li></ul>\n<p>The same temperatures work for white tea. Try them with our <a href=\"/teas/bai-mudan/\">Bai Mudan</a>, the most forgiving tea we sell.</p>\n</main>\n<footer><p>Lumen Tea Co., 18 Harbour Lane, Bristol BS1 4RW, United Kingdom \u00b7 <a href=\"/about/\">About us</a> \u00b7 <a href=\"/contact/\">Contact</a> \u00b7 <a href=\"/teas/\">All teas</a></p><p>\u00a9 2026 Lumen Tea Co.</p></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n",

Stored well, white tea keeps for years and even improves; green tea is best within six months of harvest.</p>\n<h2>The four rules</h2>\n<ul><li>Airtight: keep the leaf in its resealable pouch, pressed flat, or in a tin with a tight lid.</li>\n<li>Dark: a cupboard, not a glass jar on the counter.</li>\n<li>Cool and dry: away from the kettle, the oven and the dishwasher. Do not keep tea in the fridge; it picks up damp when you take it out.</li>\n<li>Away from smells: not next to spices, coffee or soap.</li></ul>\n<h2>How long it keeps</h2>\n<p>Green tea is at its best for six months and drinkable for a year. White tea such as Bai Mudan changes slowly over several years, getting deeper and sweeter, which is why aged white tea is prized in Fujian. Jasmine tea keeps its scent for about a year; after that the jasmine fades before the tea does.</p>\n<p>Every pouch we sell has the harvest date printed on the back, so you can tell how old your tea is.</p>\n</main>\n<footer><p>Lumen Tea Co., 18 Harbour Lane, Bristol BS1 4RW, United Kingdom \u00b7 <a href=\"/about/\">About us</a> \u00b7 <a href=\"/contact/\">Contact</a> \u00b7 <a href=\"/teas/\">All teas</a></p><p>\u00a9 2026 Lumen Tea Co.</p></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n",

We buy ours from the Lin family in Diantou. In June the buds are layered with jasmine flowers picked the same afternoon; the flowers open overnight and the tea takes up their scent. The flowers are sifted out in the morning and the process is repeated with fresh flowers seven times.</p>\n<h2>Tasting notes</h2>\n<p>Sweet and clean, with jasmine in the aroma and a long, soft finish. There is no bitterness unless the water is too hot. The second and third infusions are often the best.</p>\n<h2>How to brew</h2>\n<p>Use 3 grams of leaf for 250 ml of water at 80 \u00b0C and steep for 3 minutes. In a gaiwan, use 5 grams for 100 ml and pour off after 40 seconds, adding 15 seconds for each infusion after that.</p>\n<p>Compare it with our <a href=\"/teas/bai-mudan/\">Bai Mudan</a>, or see <a href=\"/teas/\">all our teas</a>.</p>\n</main>\n<footer><p>Lumen Tea Co., 18 Harbour Lane, Bristol BS1 4RW, United Kingdom \u00b7 <a href=\"/about/\">About us</a> \u00b7 <a href=\"/contact/\">Contact</a> \u00b7 <a href=\"/teas/\">All teas</a></p><p>\u00a9 2026 Lumen Tea Co.</p></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n",

The session's closing message

I ran the full-site audit with the installed `seo` skill, using its `seo audit` workflow and its own scoring and verification rules. It examined 13 URLs. `report.md` lists all 48 problems the tool raised, each with the page URL, severity, priority and confidence, plus the severity the underlying script gave it.

**Most serious problems the tool found (priority Critical):**
- **Blog blocked:** `robots.txt` has `Disallow: /blog/`, which blocks the whole Journal (`/blog/` and the blog post), even though both pages are in the sitemap.
- **Product pages hidden from search:** `/teas/jasmine-silver-needle/` has a "noindex" meta tag, and `/teas/bai-mudan/` sends a noindex instruction in an HTTP header (`X-Robots-Tag`).
- **Category page dropped:** `/teas/` names the homepage as its canonical page. `/shop/` redirects to it via `/store/`, so the whole product listing drops out of search.
- **Broken product structured data:** the Jasmine Silver Needle page's Product data won't parse because of trailing commas.
- **Language link to a missing page:** the homepage's Chinese-language alternate points to `/zh/`, which returns 404.

**Other findings (priority High and below):**
- The sitemap lists `/teas/white-peony/`, which returns 404.
- The blog post links "see Dragon Well" to a page that returns 404.
- The blog post has no `<title>`.
- `/guides/storing-tea/` reuses the homepage title and nothing links to it.
- `/blog/` has no mobile viewport tag, and `/contact/` has no H1.
- The remaining findings are about thin content, missing security headers, schema and E-E-A-T gaps, and having no `llms.txt`.

**Scores:** the tool's automated dashboard gives **71/100**, but it is based mostly on homepage checks. Applying the skill's own scoring rules across the whole site gives **about 12/100 (low confidence)**, mainly because the indexing problems above carry heavy penalties.

**Things to know:**
- **No speed data:** Google's PageSpeed service refused the request as rate-limited, twice. There are no Core Web Vitals numbers, so performance is marked "insufficient data", not as a site problem.
- **No screenshots:** the browser the screenshot scripts need isn't installed.
- **One check crashed:** the article-analysis script failed on a bug in the tool itself, not a site problem.
- **Two raw results set aside:** the skill's verification step discards results the evidence contradicts. One script reported a missing mobile viewport tag on 9 pages that do have it, and a "broken link" on `/contact/` turned out to be a one-off network error that cleared on retry. Both are listed in a separate notes section of `report.md`, not left out.

**Files in `~/work/output`:**
- `report.md` — the deliverable
- `FULL-AUDIT-REPORT.md` and `ACTION-PLAN.md` — the skill's required reports
- `SCRIPT-FULL-AUDIT-REPORT.md`, `SCRIPT-ACTION-PLAN.md` and `SEO-REPORT.html` — the tool's own auto-generated files
- `raw/` — every script's output and the downloaded HTML

The site was not changed.
